| NPWK19870526, leaf n4 (本港新聞), article 「清拆城寨登記順利 二千業主完成登記 兩局議員巡九龍城了解情況」, first tier | INFERRED: 呂孝端 said that by 25 May 1987 the response to owner registration was strong: 2,000 owners had registered, about half of whom lived in the City. The date comes from 'yesterday'. | "截至昨日止，他們已登記了二千名業主，其中約一半是在城寨居住" (As of yesterday they had registered 2,000 owners, about half of whom live in the City.) |
| NPWK19870526, leaf n4 (本港新聞), article 「清拆城寨登記順利 二千業主完成登記 兩局議員巡九龍城了解情況」, second tier | The 2,000 were a fifth of the estimated 10,000 owners. About 80 to 100 owners registered each day. | "佔他們估計有一萬個業主數目的五分一，而他們平均每日登記八十至一百人" (A fifth of the 10,000 owners they estimate; on average they register 80 to 100 a day.) |
| NPWK19870526, leaf n4 (本港新聞), article 「清拆城寨登記順利 二千業主完成登記 兩局議員巡九龍城了解情況」, second tier | The government estimated that about 45 per cent of the 10,000 owners lived in their own property. | "當局估計的一萬個業主中，約有百分之四十五是自住的" (Of the 10,000 owners the authorities estimate, about 45 per cent are owner-occupiers.) |
| NPWK19870526, leaf n4 (本港新聞), article 「清拆城寨登記順利 二千業主完成登記 兩局議員巡九龍城了解情況」, third tier | The government chose 500 households in the City and measured the average floor area of each unit. More than 400 were done; the rest was to finish by the end of May 1987, to help set the compensation. | "政府亦在城寨內揀了五百個住戶，量度每個單位的平均建築面積，目前已完成了四百多個" (The government also picked 500 households in the City and measured the average floor area of each unit; over 400 are done.) |
| NPWK19870526, leaf n4 (本港新聞), article 「清拆城寨登記順利 二千業主完成登記 兩局議員巡九龍城了解情況」, third tier | The compensation plan was to be set in July or August 1987. After the Executive Council and the Finance Committee, it could be published in October or November 1987. | "當局會如期在七、八月間定出賠償方案" (The authorities will set the compensation plan on schedule in July or August.) |
| NPWK19870526, leaf n4 (本港新聞), article 「清拆城寨登記順利 二千業主完成登記 兩局議員巡九龍城了解情況」, first tier | INFERRED: Six members of the Executive and Legislative Councils (伍周美蓮, 鄭漢鈞, 黃宏發, 張有興, 潘志輝 and 廖烈科) toured Kowloon City on 25 May 1987. They viewed the district from the roof of Block 22, Tung Tau Estate (東頭村廿二座), and heard District Officer 方志偉 and then 呂孝端. | "他們曾先抵達東頭村廿二座天台俯瞰九龍城區的發展" (They first went to the roof of Block 22, Tung Tau Estate, to look down on the development of Kowloon City district.) |
| NPWK19870526, leaf n4 (本港新聞), editorial column 小評 「九龍城寨無牌醫生 善用才能勿加歧視」, first paragraph | The editorial said that the City had more than 30,000 people and about 10,000 owners, and that every registered resident would get fair compensation and rehousing. | "九龍城寨共有人口三萬多人，業主約一萬戶" (Kowloon Walled City has more than 30,000 people, and about 10,000 owner households.) |
| NPWK19870526, leaf n4 (本港新聞), editorial column 小評 「九龍城寨無牌醫生 善用才能勿加歧視」, first paragraph | INFERRED: The Governor, Sir David Wilson (衛奕信), went to Kowloon City in the week before but did not go into the City itself. | "港督衛奕信爵士上週曾到九龍城，雖然並未親自進入九龍城寨實地視察" (Governor Sir David Wilson went to Kowloon City last week, though he did not go into the Walled City himself to inspect it.) |
| NPWK19870526, leaf n4 (本港新聞), editorial column 小評 「九龍城寨無牌醫生 善用才能勿加歧視」, first paragraph | INFERRED: Asked about the unlicensed doctors of the City, the Governor said that the government would help them in various ways to use their skills in lawful work. | "港督答稱港府計劃以各種方法，協助他們運用技能，善用其才能做一些合法的工作" (The Governor answered that the government plans in various ways to help them use their skills in lawful work.) |
| NPWK19870526, leaf n4 (本港新聞), editorial column 小評 「九龍城寨無牌醫生 善用才能勿加歧視」, second part | The editorial said that the doctors and dentists of Chinese and Western medicine in the City were unlicensed, and that the clearance would hit them hardest. | "按照本港法例，無牌醫生不能正式執業，因此遷拆，這些無牌醫生所受的打擊最大" (Under Hong Kong law unlicensed doctors cannot practise formally, thus in the clearance these unlicensed doctors are hit hardest.) |
| NPWK19870526, leaf n4 (本港新聞), editorial column 小評 「九龍城寨無牌醫生 善用才能勿加歧視」, second part | The editorial said that the unlicensed doctors had practised in the City for a long time and still made a living, thus there was a need for them. | "無牌醫生長期在城寨執業，仍能維持其生計，存在有其需要" (Unlicensed doctors have long practised in the City and still make a living; their existence answers a need.) |

- **Leads:**
  - The owner registration figures of the Registrar General's Department, May 1987 (not held).
  - The government sample survey of the floor area of 500 households of the City, 1987 (not held).
### zh-press-7: 九龍城寨牙醫希望取得一視同仁地位

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-17; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-19.
- **Citation:** 華僑日報 (Wah Kiu Yat Po), 「牙醫管理委會定九零年開始 牙醫註冊執業要參與評審考試」 with the sub-article 「九龍城寨牙醫希望取得一視同仁地位」, 2 June 1987, 第二張第二頁 (本港新聞), archive.org item NPWK19870602, leaf n5, https://archive.org/details/NPWK19870602
- **Class:** primary (A news report of statements made the day before by the vice-chairman of the City's kaifong association and by dental officials, made at the time (1987). Primary tree is correct.)
- **Date of the record:** 1987-06-02
- **Files:**
  - `zh-press-7-NPWK19870602_jp2_NPWK19870602_0005.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Full-resolution page image of leaf n5 (第二張第二頁, 本港新聞), the page with the lead story and the City sub-article, as archive.org serves it in JPEG from the JP2 scan.; https://archive.org/download/NPWK19870602/NPWK19870602_jp2.zip/NPWK19870602_jp2%2FNPWK19870602_0005.jp2&ext=jpg; 2536385 bytes; SHA-256 `26888ebc4ada34735924cdb31f022d249dc0c1fd7b1215463985e46db5a7cc59`; licence: No rights statement on the archive.org item (its metadata fields rights and licenseurl are empty; it is in the collection china-mail, from a private uploader, with no source named). The newspaper is copyright of its publisher, 華僑日報 (Wah Kiu Yat Po). (https://archive.org/details/NPWK19870602)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| NPWK19870602, leaf n5 (本港新聞), lead article 「牙醫管理委會定九零年開始 牙醫註冊執業要參與評審考試」, first tier | The Dental Council (牙醫管理委員會) had decided that from April 1990 all dental graduates, local, Mainland or overseas, had to pass a licensing examination before they could register to practise in Hong Kong. | "規定所有牙科畢業學生，不論是本地，國內及海外的，若要在本港註冊執業，一律都要參與一個執業評審考試" (It requires all dental graduates, whether local, Mainland or overseas, who want to register to practise in Hong Kong, to sit a licensing examination.) |
| NPWK19870602, leaf n5 (本港新聞), sub-article 「九龍城寨牙醫希望取得一視同仁地位」, first part | 陳協平, vice-chairman of the City's kaifong welfare association (九龍城寨街坊福利會), welcomed the rule that all dental graduates must pass an examination. He saw it as equal treatment with Commonwealth graduates. | "九龍城寨街坊福利會副會長陳協平表示歡迎。他認為港府此項措施顯示今後對英聯邦牙科畢業生一視同仁" (Vice-chairman of the Kowloon Walled City kaifong welfare association 陳協平 welcomed it; he thinks this measure shows equal treatment of Commonwealth dental graduates from now on.) |
| NPWK19870602, leaf n5 (本港新聞), sub-article 「九龍城寨牙醫希望取得一視同仁地位」, second part | 陳協平 said that the City's dental clinics helped with the shortage of dentists in Hong Kong. Each clinic treated three patients a day by a low count, thus they treated more than 200 patients a day in all. | "即使以每所診所每日服務三名病人計算。每日亦解決了二百多名牙科病人的問題" (Even counting each clinic as serving three patients a day, they still deal with over 200 dental patients a day.) |
| NPWK19870602, leaf n5 (本港新聞), sub-article 「九龍城寨牙醫希望取得一視同仁地位」, second part | He asked for an outlet for the clinic owners who had learned as apprentices or from their families, as well as the examination for graduates, so that they could go on serving the public. | "對其他學徒出身或祖傳的牙科診所主持人應給予出路" (For other dental clinic operators who trained as apprentices or by family tradition, an outlet should be given.) |
| NPWK19870602, leaf n5 (本港新聞), sub-article 「九龍城寨牙醫希望取得一視同仁地位」, last part | He said that most of these dentists had real skill and a fair professional standard, and kept to their limits: they usually did only fillings and dentures. For extractions they mostly sent patients to other dentists. | "一般只進行鑲牙、補牙手術，至於脫牙手術，則多會轉介與其他牙醫" (Generally they do only dentures and fillings; for extractions they mostly refer patients to other dentists.) |

- **Leads:**
  - The decision of the Dental Council (牙醫管理委員會) of 1987 that all dental graduates must pass a licensing examination from April 1990 (not held).
### top-zh-press-9: 昨與浦炳榮等特別委員座談城寨牙醫盼續營生以鑲牙補牙洗牙為主集體經營

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-17; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-19.
- **Citation:** Ta Kung Pao (大公報), 「昨與浦炳榮等特別委員座談 城寨牙醫盼續營生 以鑲牙補牙洗牙為主集體經營」, 10 June 1987, p.5 (港聞), archive.org item NPTKP19870610, leaf n4, https://archive.org/details/NPTKP19870610/page/n4
- **Class:** primary (A news report of the time (1987), written one day after the events it reports. It belongs in primary-sources/.)
- **Date of the record:** 1987-06-10

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| NPTKP19870610, leaf n4 (printed p.5, 港聞), lower middle | INFERRED: On 9 June 1987 several members of the special committee met the dentists of the City at the kaifong association, to hear their views on the clearance. The date comes from 'yesterday'. | "前往九龍城寨街坊會，與城寨內的牙醫舉行座談會" (Went to the Kowloon Walled City Kaifong Association and held a forum with the dentists of the City.) |
| NPTKP19870610, leaf n4 (printed p.5, 港聞), lower middle | All the dentists who spoke hoped to keep their work. They asked to work outside the City as 'denture makers' (鑲牙師). | "建議港府准許他們以「鑲牙師」的身份在區外營業" (Proposed that the government let them practise outside the area as 'denture makers'.) |
| NPTKP19870610, leaf n4 (printed p.5, 港聞), lower middle | After the clearance announcement the dentists of the City formed a dentists' group (牙醫組). Its leader was 李德康. | "九龍城寨的牙醫在港府宣佈清拆城寨後組成了牙醫組" (The dentists of the City formed a dentists' group after the government announced the clearance.) |
| NPTKP19870610, leaf n4 (printed p.5, 港聞), lower middle | The group surveyed the dentists of the City. Most wanted to go on working, and more than 90 per cent wanted to go on serving the residents with their present skills. | "其中九成以上希望以他們現時所長，為區內居民繼續服務" (Over 90 per cent of them hope to keep serving the area's residents with their present skills.) |
| NPTKP19870610, leaf n4 (printed p.5, 港聞), lower middle | Most dentists in the City mainly made dentures. They also did some simple extractions, fillings and cleaning. | "現時城寨內大部分牙醫的主要業務是鑲牙" (At present the main business of most dentists in the City is making dentures.) |
| NPTKP19870610, leaf n4 (printed p.5, 港聞), lower middle | For more difficult work, the dentists of the City sent patients to registered dentists whom they knew. | "較複雜的手術，他們便會轉介紹給相熟的註冊牙醫" (For more complex procedures they refer patients to registered dentists they know.) |
| NPTKP19870610, leaf n4 (printed p.5, 港聞), lower middle | 李德康 said the City's dentists charged low fees and kept a code of practice: they did no complex operations. | "城寨的牙醫收費低廉，亦有遵守一定的經營守則" (The City's dentists charge low fees and also keep a certain code of practice.) |
| NPTKP19870610, leaf n4 (printed p.5, 港聞), lower middle | The dentists would welcome a place given by the government where they could work together under supervision. | "假如港府撥出一個地點讓他們集中經營，以便監管，他們是歡迎的" (If the government gave a site for them to work together, for supervision, they would welcome it.) |
| NPTKP19870610, leaf n4 (printed p.5, 港聞), lower middle | Some dentists proposed that ten or more of them work together and share the cost of one registered dentist for complex operations. | "將十多名同業集中一起經營" (Ten-odd colleagues work together in one place.) |
| NPTKP19870610, leaf n4 (printed p.5, 港聞), lower middle | 呂孝端 said dental services in Hong Kong were under very strict control. He could not answer at once, but would pass the proposals to the Medical and Health Department (醫務衞生署). | "現時本港的牙醫服務受到非常嚴謹的管制" (At present dental services in Hong Kong are under very strict control.) |
| NPTKP19870610, leaf n4 (printed p.5, 港聞), lower middle | The members at the meeting were 浦炳榮, 梁煒彤, 簡福飴, 陳協平 and 鄧泰全. The officials were 呂孝端, 毛燦明 (屋宇地政署副署長) and 陳德平 (房屋署助理署長). | "屋宇地政署副署長毛燦明及房屋署助理署長陳德平等" (毛燦明, deputy director of Buildings and Lands, and 陳德平, assistant director of Housing, and others.) |

- **Leads:**
  - Opinion survey of the City's dentists by their dentists' group (牙醫組), early 1987 (cited in 大公報 10 Jun 1987, leaf n4; not held).
### top-services-3: 城寨供水商晤清拆委員要求每口井賠償廿萬元答應繼續供水直至城寨居民全部遷出為止目前區內有水井六十口清拆委會明討論 (大公報 1987-06-19)

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-17; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-19.

- **Class:** primary (A news report made the day after the visit (1987), with a reporter's feature of the same day. The feature's history of the 1950s-60s is secondary, and the facts from it are marked so. The item belongs in primary-sources/.)
- **Date of the record:** 1987-06-19
- **Files:**
  - `top-services-3-n6.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Page image (two printed pages) of 大公報 19 Jun 1987: the wells article and the feature 「擔水婆與供水商」; https://archive.org/download/NPTKP19870619/page/n6.jpg; 3974282 bytes; SHA-256 `0ebbc6dee639d6c1b8dfa235a700107ed9a20f68ae55ffa48a6465a3d75db3de`; licence: No rights statement on the archive.org item (uploaded scan in the collection 'china-mail'); copyright of the publisher (https://archive.org/details/NPTKP19870619)
- **Note:** its 4 secondary facts (narration, later captions) are in the secondary twin, [`../../secondary-sources/hk-chinese-press-1985-1993/SOURCE.md`](../../secondary-sources/hk-chinese-press-1985-1993/SOURCE.md).

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| NPTKP19870619, leaf n6, right half (大公報 港聞 page), top right | INFERRED: On 18 June 1987 the special committee on the clearance (九龍城寨清拆事宜特別委員會) visited the City and met the well owners and water suppliers. The date comes from 'yesterday'. | "九龍城寨清拆事宜特別委員會昨日前往城寨訪問" (The Kowloon Walled City clearance special committee visited the City yesterday.) |
| NPTKP19870619, leaf n6, right half (大公報 港聞 page), top right | The suppliers' representatives promised to supply water until all residents had left. They asked HK$200,000 of compensation for each well. | "答應會繼續供水至城寨居民完全遷出為止，並要求港府對每口水井賠償二十萬元" (Promised to keep supplying water until the residents all move out, and asked the government for 200,000 dollars for each well.) |
| NPTKP19870619, leaf n6, right half (大公報 港聞 page), top right | The visitors were the committee members 浦炳榮, Chan Hip Ping (陳協平; the English name is from the repo's other sources) and 鄧泰全, with 副政務司 呂孝端, 首席助理政務司 苗華安, the Kowloon City district police commander 王梁錦珊, and Buildings and Lands and Housing officers. | "特別委員會委員包括浦炳榮、陳協平及鄧泰全" (The special committee members included 浦炳榮, 陳協平 and 鄧泰全.) |
| NPTKP19870619, leaf n6, right half (大公報 港聞 page), top right | The committee first looked at four of the wells in the City, then held a meeting with residents' representatives. | "首先往城寨區內其中四口水井，然後與居民代表舉行座談會" (First went to four of the wells in the City, then held a meeting with residents' representatives.) |
| NPTKP19870619, leaf n6, right half (大公報 港聞 page), top right | A spokesman of the Kowloon Walled City Kai Fong Association (九龍城寨街坊福利會) said that the association had agreed the claim with the well merchants (水井商). | "福利會與水井商經協商之後" (After talks between the welfare association and the well merchants.) |
| NPTKP19870619, leaf n6, right half (大公報 港聞 page), top right | Of the HK$200,000 per well, HK$80,000 was for the sinking of the well and the supply equipment. HK$120,000 was for the losses when the well stopped, such as staff severance pay and the merchants' change of trade. | "其中八萬元用以賠償水井開鑿及供水設備，另外十二萬元則用以賠償因水井停止供水而導至的損失" (Of which 80,000 compensates the sinking of the well and the supply equipment, and 120,000 the losses from the well stopping supply.) |
| NPTKP19870619, leaf n6, right half (大公報 港聞 page), top right | The well businesses had employees (員工): severance pay was part of the claim. | "如員工的遣散費及商人的轉業賠償等" (Such as staff severance pay and compensation for the merchants changing trade.) |
| NPTKP19870619, leaf n6, right half (大公報 港聞 page), top right | In June 1987 the City had about 60 wells. | "現時城寨約共有六十口水井" (At present the City has about sixty wells.) |
| NPTKP19870619, leaf n6, right half (大公報 港聞 page), top right | The suppliers said they would repair any broken equipment until the end, and they stressed that they would not raise the water charge. | "他們亦會負責維修，並強調不會增加水費" (They will also be responsible for repairs, and stressed that they will not raise water charges.) |
| NPTKP19870619, leaf n6, right half (大公報 港聞 page), top right | 呂孝端 said that the committee had met the City's owners, doctors, dentists, shops, factories and water suppliers. The government was writing a report for the committee meeting of the next day (20 June 1987). | "特別委員會已經先後與城寨的業主、醫生、牙醫、商戶、廠戶及供水商接觸" (The special committee has met in turn the City's owners, doctors, dentists, shops, factories and water suppliers.) |
| NPTKP19870619, leaf n6, right half (大公報 港聞 page), top right | The district police commander 王梁錦珊, a woman, said that crime in the City fell about 20 per cent in the four months after the clearance announcement of 14 January 1987, against the same months of 1986. | "城寨的整體罪案有顯著下降的趨勢，比去年同期下跌約百分之二十" (Overall crime in the City shows a clear fall, down about 20 per cent on the same period last year.) |
| NPTKP19870619, leaf n6, right half (大公報 港聞 page), top right | Burglaries in the City fell from 62 in the first four months of 1986 to 12 in the first four months of 1987. The paper gives the fall as 74.1 per cent, but the two numbers give 80.6 per cent. | "由去年同期的六十二宗跌至今年首四個月的十二宗，降幅達百分之七十四點一" (From 62 cases in the same period last year to 12 in the first four months of this year, a fall of 74.1 per cent.) |
| NPTKP19870619, leaf n6, left half (港聞・文教, 第七版), top left: 特稿 by 本報記者 蕭錦行 | The Water Supplies Department (水務局) never supplied the City with drinking water, so a trade of water suppliers grew up. | "九龍城寨卻一直未獲水務局供應食水，因而出現了一班食水供應商" (The Walled City never got drinking water from the Water Supplies Department, so a group of water suppliers appeared.) |
| NPTKP19870619, leaf n6, left half (港聞・文教, 第七版), top left: 特稿 by 本報記者 蕭錦行 | In 1987 the City had about 60 such deep wells. They were usually about 300 feet deep, and residents drew the water with pumps. | "這些深水井一般深度約三百呎，居民主要以水泵汲取食水使用" (These deep wells are usually about 300 feet deep; residents mainly draw the water with pumps.) |
| NPTKP19870619, leaf n6, left half (港聞・文教, 第七版), top left: 特稿 by 本報記者 蕭錦行 | Some people dug wells as a trade, connected water pipes to homes, supplied water and charged a 'water fee' (水費). The feature calls them the only water-supply trade in Hong Kong. | "為居民接駁水管及供應食水，並收取費用作為「水費」" (Connected water pipes for residents and supplied water, charging a fee as 'water charges'.) |

- **Leads:**
  - Papers of the special committee meeting of 20 June 1987 on the compensation of the wells (Housing Authority), not held.
### gap-clearance-from-below-10: 大公報 1987-09-13: 城寨業主聯誼會昨表示補價不應低於市區居屋市區重建補償方案可作為參考

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-17; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-19.
- **Citation:** Ta Kung Pao (大公報), 「城寨業主聯誼會昨表示 補價不應低於市區居屋 市區重建補償方案可作為參考」, 13 September 1987, p. 5 (港聞), archive.org item NPTKP19870913, leaf n4, https://archive.org/details/NPTKP19870913/page/n4
- **Class:** primary (A newspaper report of the next day on the words of the chairman of an owners' body of the City (1987). It belongs in primary-sources/.)
- **Date of the record:** 1987-09-13

  - `gap-clearance-from-below-10-BookReaderImages.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Full-resolution JPEG of leaf n4 of the archive.org scan of 大公報 13 Sep 1987 (the page with the article), about 4-6 MB. Item page: https://archive.org/details/NPTKP19870913/page/n4; https://ia800708.us.archive.org/BookReader/BookReaderImages.php?zip=/3/items/NPTKP19870913/NPTKP19870913_jp2.zip&file=NPTKP19870913_jp2/NPTKP19870913_0004.jp2&id=NPTKP19870913&scale=1&rotate=0; 4592833 bytes; SHA-256 `598c8b53435861afc6610165e0e151fbc6497a3f8f3bd84e2a09eee7d3fb294b`; licence: No rights statement: the archive.org item has empty 'rights' and 'licenseurl' fields (uploaded by a private person, collections 'china-mail' and 'newspapers'). Copyright of the publisher. (https://archive.org/details/NPTKP19870913)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| NPTKP19870913, leaf n4 (第五版 港聞), top left | INFERRED: The chairman of the owners' fellowship association of the City (九龍城寨業主聯誼會) spoke at a members' dinner on the evening of 12 September 1987. The date comes from 'last night'. His name is 伍錫陸 (clear on the full-resolution scan). | "九龍城寨業主聯誼會主席" (Chairman of the Kowloon Walled City owners' fellowship association.) |
| NPTKP19870913, leaf n4 (第五版 港聞), top left | He spoke about the four principles for the compensation of owners that the special committee on the clearance had put forward. | "就城寨清拆特別委員會提出業主補償方案的四項原則發表意見" (Gave his views on the four principles of the owners' compensation scheme put forward by the special committee on the clearance.) |
| NPTKP19870913, leaf n4 (第五版 港聞), top left | The special committee had put forward the four principles on 25 August 1987. | "九龍城寨清拆特別委員會在八月二十五日，曾提出補償方案的四項原則" (On 25 August the special committee on the Kowloon Walled City clearance put forward four principles for the compensation scheme.) |
| NPTKP19870913, leaf n4 (第五版 港聞), top left | He said that compensation must be based on the market price of private property near the City, and must not be lower than the recent price of urban Home Ownership Scheme (居屋) flats. | "有關的補償計算應以城寨鄰近地區私人物業的市價為根據，並以不低於近期市區居屋的售價為原則" (Compensation should be calculated on the market price of private property near the City, on the principle of not being lower than recent urban HOS sale prices.) |
| NPTKP19870913, leaf n4 (第五版 港聞), top left | He said that the urban-renewal compensation scheme that the Land Development Corporation (土地發展公司) had recently proposed could be a model for the owners of the City. | "土地發展公司最近提出的市區重建補償方案可作城寨業主補償的參考" (The urban redevelopment compensation scheme recently proposed by the Land Development Corporation can serve as a reference for the compensation of City owners.) |
| NPTKP19870913, leaf n4 (第五版 港聞), top left | He said that compensation must not be based on the market price of property inside the City, because property in the City sold at prices different from other private buildings. | "因為城寨內物業的買賣價有別於其他私人樓宇" (Because the sale prices of property in the City differ from those of other private buildings.) |
| NPTKP19870913, leaf n4 (第五版 港聞), top left | He said that the compensation of an owner might not be enough to buy a private flat or shop of the same size in the nearby area. | "城寨業主所得的補償額可能不足以購買鄰近地區同樣面積的私人物業住宅或舖位" (The compensation City owners get may not be enough to buy a private flat or shop of the same size in the nearby area.) |
| NPTKP19870913, leaf n4 (第五版 港聞), top left | After hearing its members, the association was to give the owners' views to the clearance office (城寨清拆專責辦事處) on Monday 14 September 1987, for the special committee's meeting on Tuesday 15 September. | "將於星期一（十四日）將各業主意見交城寨清拆專責辦事處" (Will hand the owners' views to the City clearance special office on Monday (the 14th).) |
| NPTKP19870913, leaf n4, middle | A short report on the same page said that a visiting group of young students from Jiangsu (江蘇青年學生訪問團), invited by 學友社, was to see the MTR, the airport, public housing estates and the City, among other sights. | "地下鐵路、機場、公屋、九龍城寨及風景名勝等" (The underground railway, the airport, public housing, Kowloon Walled City and scenic spots, etc.) |

- **Leads:**
  - The four principles of compensation for owners, put forward by 九龍城寨清拆事宜特別委員會 on 25 August 1987 (not held).
  - The owners' views that 九龍城寨業主聯誼會 gave to 城寨清拆專責辦事處 on 14 September 1987, and the special committee's meeting of 15 September 1987 (not held).
  - The plan notes that the Landowners Association files that the repo holds (HKRS1077-2-2, HKRS1078-1-82) can be of the same body; this article does not give an English name, so the match is not confirmed.
### zh-press-9: 城寨清拆賠償方案 現等待行政局通過 / 九龍城寨街坊會 祝廿五週年聯歡

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-17; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-19.

- **Class:** primary (News reports made at the time (1987) of an official's report to the District Board and of the kaifong association's anniversary party, with speeches. Primary tree is correct.)

| NPWK19870926, leaf n26 (第三張第七頁, 本港新聞), article 「城寨清拆賠償方案 現等待行政局通過 料年底公布明年中第一批居民遷出」, first tier | INFERRED: 呂孝端 told the Kowloon City District Board on 24 September 1987 that the special committee had finished a recommended plan for the City. It waited for the Executive Council and the LegCo Finance Committee. The date comes from 'the day before yesterday'. | "呂孝端前日出席九龍城區議會時指出，九龍城寨清拆特別委員會已完成一項方案" (呂孝端, at the Kowloon City District Board the day before yesterday, said that the special committee on the clearance had finished a plan.) |
| NPWK19870926, leaf n26 (第三張第七頁, 本港新聞), article 「城寨清拆賠償方案 現等待行政局通過 料年底公布明年中第一批居民遷出」, first tier | The full plan was expected to be published at the end of 1987, and the first batch of City residents was to move out in mid-1988. | "料年底可公佈整個方案的內容。明年中將有第一批城寨居民遷出" (The whole plan is expected to be published at year end; in the middle of next year the first batch of City residents will move out.) |
| NPWK19870926, leaf n26 (第三張第七頁, 本港新聞), article 「城寨清拆賠償方案 現等待行政局通過 料年底公布明年中第一批居民遷出」, second tier | The committee was to set a deadline for owners who had not registered their title, before the plan was published. Later registration needed the committee's approval. | "特委會將定出限期辦理，會在推薦方案公佈之前截止" (The special committee will set a deadline, closing before the recommended plan is published.) |
| NPWK19870926, leaf n26 (第三張第七頁, 本港新聞), article 「城寨清拆賠償方案 現等待行政局通過 料年底公布明年中第一批居民遷出」, second and third tiers | 7,221 owners had registered, 87 per cent of the 8,300 owners of the City; 13 per cent had not. The Registrar General expected over 90 per cent by the end of September 1987. | "數目為七千二百二十一，而城寨的業主總數為八千三百" (The number is 7,221, and the total number of owners in the City is 8,300.) |
| NPWK19870926, leaf n26 (第三張第七頁, 本港新聞), article 「城寨清拆賠償方案 現等待行政局通過 料年底公布明年中第一批居民遷出」, third tier | INFERRED: The Housing Department's re-check was to focus on the residents of the first phase. The Buildings and Lands Department had measured the floor area of 900 households. | "屋宇地政處已為九百戶居民量度屋宇面積" (The Buildings and Lands Department has measured the floor area for 900 households.) |
| NPWK19870926, leaf n26 (第三張第七頁, 本港新聞), article 「城寨清拆賠償方案 現等待行政局通過 料年底公布明年中第一批居民遷出」, third tier | Resident owners could choose their compensation: money to buy a Home Ownership Scheme flat, or a public rental flat. Non-resident owners were to get cash only. | "即償款購買居屋或分配公屋：非自住業主則指有現金的補償" (That is, compensation money to buy an HOS flat or allocation of public housing; non-resident owners get only cash compensation.) |
| NPWK19870926, leaf n26 (第三張第七頁, 本港新聞), article 「城寨清拆賠償方案 現等待行政局通過 料年底公布明年中第一批居民遷出」, third tier, left | 苗華安 said that the clearance office had added staff to visit the City in the evening and explain the plan to residents. They were also making arrangements with the City's five service bodies. | "專責事務處已增加工作員在城寨作晚間的探訪，向居民解釋有關事宜" (The special office has added staff to make evening visits in the City and explain matters to residents.) |
| NPWK19870926, leaf n26 (第三張第七頁, 本港新聞), article 「城寨清拆賠償方案 現等待行政局通過 料年底公布明年中第一批居民遷出」, third tier, left end | INFERRED: The Kowloon City district commander (九龍城區指揮官; the name reads as 梁錦珊, with one unclear character before it) said that 24 robberies had happened in the City up to the end of August 1987, slightly fewer than in the same period of 1986. | "截至上月止，城寨共發生二十四宗劫案，比去年同期略減" (Up to last month, 24 robberies in all took place in the City, slightly fewer than in the same period last year.) |
| NPWK19870926, leaf n26 (第三張第七頁), article 「九龍城寨街坊會 祝廿五週年聯歡 副政務司呂孝端主禮」, first tier | INFERRED: The City's kaifong association (九龍城寨街坊福利事業促進委員會) held its 25th-anniversary party at 8 p.m. on 23 September 1987 at the restaurant 海洋皇宮酒樓. Thus it was founded about 1962. | "廿三日下午八時，假海洋皇宮酒樓慶祝廿五週年聯歡大會" (On the 23rd at 8 p.m., at 海洋皇宮酒樓, it celebrated its 25th-anniversary party.) |
| NPWK19870926, leaf n26 (第三張第七頁), article 「九龍城寨街坊會 祝廿五週年聯歡 副政務司呂孝端主禮」, second tier | INFERRED: The chairman of the association (name read as 李偉霖; the last character is not clear) said that the special committee and officials had gone deep into the City that year, met residents and heard their views. | "深入城寨，接觸居民，聽取意見，使賠償安置等工作的研究得以順利進行" (They went deep into the City, met residents and heard views, so that the study of compensation and rehousing could go smoothly.) |
| NPWK19870926, leaf n26 (第三張第七頁), article 「九龍城寨街坊會 祝廿五週年聯歡 副政務司呂孝端主禮」, first tier | Vice-chairman 陳協平 asked for flat-for-flat compensation for residential titles at no less than the value of an urban Home Ownership flat, and looser residence rules for public housing. | "應給予樓換樓，而賠償標準應不低於市區居屋的價值" (They should be given a flat for a flat, and the compensation standard should not be lower than the value of an urban HOS flat.) |
| NPWK19870926, leaf n26 (第三張第七頁), article 「九龍城寨街坊會 祝廿五週年聯歡 副政務司呂孝端主禮」, first tier | He also asked that shops and factories get enough compensation to carry on their trades. 呂孝端 said that both points were reasonable. | "其所得的賠償應使其能夠繼續營生樂業" (Their compensation should let them carry on making a living in their trade.) |
| NPWK19870926, leaf n26 (第三張第七頁), article 「九龍城寨街坊會 祝廿五週年聯歡 副政務司呂孝端主禮」, first tier, left | Guests included 呂孝端, 喬宗淮 (deputy secretary-general, Xinhua Hong Kong branch), 周德熙 (Director of City and New Territories Administration), 簡福貽, LegCo member 謝志偉 and Urban Councillor 陳子鈞. | "新華社香港分社副秘書長喬宗淮" (Deputy secretary-general of the Xinhua News Agency Hong Kong branch, 喬宗淮.) |

- **Leads:**
  - The minutes of the Kowloon City District Board meeting of 24 September 1987, with the report of 呂孝端 and the police figures of 24 robberies in the City up to August 1987 (not held).
  - The owner registration figures of the Registrar General's Department, September 1987 (7,221 of 8,300 owners) (not held).
### top-services-21: 四年內執行分三期清拆當局撥款二十七億賠償安置城寨居民 (大公報 1987-12-11)

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-17; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-19.
- **Citation:** Ta Kung Pao (大公報), 「四年內執行分三期清拆 當局撥款二十七億賠償安置城寨居民 業主將可獲現金補償及優先購居屋」, 11 December 1987; with 「情況特殊優惠照顧 胡法光說下不為例 色情塲所及賭檔無資格獲補償」, 「城寨福利會陳協平稱賠償方案可以接受但希望照顧牙醫日後執業出路」, 「五千個公屋單位為城寨居民準備」 and a map, all on the same page, archive.org item NPTKP19871211, leaf n4, https://archive.org/details/NPTKP19871211/page/n4
- **Class:** primary (A Hong Kong daily newspaper report printed the day after the events it reports. It belongs in primary-sources/.)
- **Date of the record:** 1987-12-11
- **Files:**
  - `top-services-21-n4.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Full-resolution scan of leaf n4 (港聞 page) of Ta Kung Pao (大公報), 11 December 1987, with the four City articles, the three-phase map and two photos (JPEG).; https://archive.org/download/NPTKP19871211/page/n4.jpg; 3824345 bytes; SHA-256 `5065de26ff8b8b2bbef0ca302a0f5d3b694096d3ff5e37cb3c401333899bb7e5`; licence: No licence or rights statement: the archive.org item metadata has no 'rights' and no 'licenseurl'. The newspaper is copyright of Ta Kung Pao (大公報). (https://archive.org/details/NPTKP19871211)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, main article | INFERRED: On 10 December 1987 the special committee for the clearance announced that the Finance Committee of the Legislative Council had approved HK$2,760 million for the compensation and rehousing of the City. The date comes from 'yesterday'. | "立法局財務委員會已通過撥出二十七億六千萬元" (The Legislative Council Finance Committee has approved 2,760 million dollars.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, main article | Hu Fa-kuang (胡法光), chairman of the committee, said HK$2,680 million of it was for the owners of the City. The rest was for demolition, security and other costs. The money was to go to the Housing Authority over five years. | "大部分是用作對城寨業主的賠償，共計達二十六億八千萬元" (Most is for compensation of City owners, totalling 2,680 million dollars.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, main article | The plan separated three things in the City: ownership, the right of occupation, and industry and commerce. | "將九龍城寨的業權、住權和工商業分開" (To separate the ownership, occupation rights and industry and commerce of Kowloon Walled City.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, main article | Every holder of a title in the City could get cash equal to the fair market value of the flat or to the value of a Home Ownership Scheme flat. The owner could also rent public housing, buy an HOS flat with priority, or get an interest-free loan. | "可獲得一筆相當於公平市值或居屋市值的現金賠償" (Can get a cash compensation equal to the fair market value or the HOS market value.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4, box 賠償辦法 | An eligible tenant could rent a public housing flat, buy an HOS flat with priority, or get an interest-free loan of the future Home Purchase Loan Scheme to buy a private flat. | "符合資格的租客，可選擇租住公屋單位，或優先購置居屋" (Eligible tenants may choose to rent a public housing unit, or buy an HOS flat with priority.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, main article | INFERRED: The committee met representatives of the City's kaifong (街坊) on the morning of 10 December 1987 to explain the plan. | "清拆委員會昨日上午與城寨街坊代表舉行會議" (The clearance committee met City kaifong representatives yesterday morning.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, main article | The plan did not yet settle the future of the unregistered doctors and dentists of the City. | "城寨內非註冊醫生及牙醫日後的安排則暫時未有定案" (The future arrangements for unregistered doctors and dentists in the City are not yet decided.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, main article | Hu Fa-kuang (胡法光) said the terms were generous because of the special history of the City, and were not a precedent for other clearances. | "並不能作為日後清拆賠償的先例" (Cannot be a precedent for future clearance compensation.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, sub-article 「情況特殊優惠照顧 胡法光說下不為例」 | Hu Fa-kuang (胡法光) said the property prices of the City were low. A subsidy to buy private flats would cost too much, so the help was based on HOS prices. | "鑑於城寨物業價格偏低" (Because property prices in the City are low.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, sub-article 「情況特殊優惠照顧 胡法光說下不為例」 | The committee named food factories as an example of a City trade that could get compensation. | "例如食品工廠這些應可獲補償資格的行業" (For example food factories, trades that should be eligible for compensation.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, sub-article 「情況特殊優惠照顧 胡法光說下不為例」 | Vice establishments and gambling stalls in the City could get no compensation. | "色情場所及賭檔，因為這些行業並無資格獲得補償" (Vice establishments and gambling stalls, because these trades are not eligible for compensation.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, sub-article 「情況特殊優惠照顧 胡法光說下不為例」 | The cut-off date was 14 January 1987. A person who moved into the City after it, or who divided a flat, built, extended or started a business after it, got no compensation. An owner who bought after it got only the fair market value. | "凡於一九八七年一月十四日以後才入住城寨的人士" (Anyone who moved into the City only after 14 January 1987.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, sub-article 「情況特殊優惠照顧 胡法光說下不為例」 | Eligible residents and businesses were to get a removal allowance at standard rates. | "將獲發按標準補償率計算的搬遷津貼" (Will receive a removal allowance calculated at the standard compensation rate.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, sub-article 「情況特殊優惠照顧 胡法光說下不為例」 | There were five voluntary organizations in the City. The Social Welfare Department found new premises for those that had a government subsidy; the others got no new premises. | "城寨內共有五個志願團體" (There are five voluntary organizations in the City in all.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, sub-article 「情況特殊優惠照顧 胡法光說下不為例」 | Commercial well-water suppliers (商業性水井供水商) worked in the City in December 1987. They were to go on supplying water until all residents had left. | "城寨供水商將會繼續供水服務，直至所有居民遷出城寨為止" (The City's water suppliers will continue their supply service until all residents have moved out of the City.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, sub-article 「情況特殊優惠照顧 胡法光說下不為例」 | The City and New Territories Administration (政務總署) and the Buildings and Lands Department (屋宇地政署) had met the well-water suppliers several times to hear their views on compensation. | "曾與供水商舉行多次會議" (Held several meetings with the water suppliers.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, sub-article 「情況特殊優惠照顧 胡法光說下不為例」 | Staff of the City and New Territories Administration (政務總署) and of the Social Welfare Department (社會福利署) had visited the old people of the City and others who needed help. | "職員已探訪城寨的老人家及其他需要幫助的人士" (Staff have visited the City's old people and others who need help.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, sub-article 「情況特殊優惠照顧 胡法光說下不為例」 | Two panels heard complaints: an appeal panel for clearance matters, and an assessment review panel for compensation assessments. | "上訴審裁小組" (Appeal tribunal panel.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, sub-article 「情況特殊優惠照顧 胡法光說下不為例」 | The government planned to clear the City in three phases, from December 1987 to 1990/91. | "分三期清拆九龍城寨" (Clear Kowloon Walled City in three phases.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, sub-article 「情況特殊優惠照顧 胡法光說下不為例」 | The letters to the residents of Phase 1 were to go out at the end of December 1987. There were more than 3,000 letters, so they went out in batches. | "需要發出的專函超過三千封" (More than 3,000 letters need to be sent.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), top half, sub-article 「情況特殊優惠照顧 胡法光說下不為例」 | The first resident of Phase 1 was expected to move to a new home at about the Lunar New Year of 1988. | "預料約於即將來臨的農曆新年期間遷入新的居所" (Expected to move into the new home around the coming Lunar New Year.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), map 「城寨分三期清拆的分區略圖」 | The map divides the City into three clearance areas: Phase 1 in the west, cleared in 1988; Phase 2 in the middle, 1989; Phase 3 in the east, 1990. | "第一期 八八年清拆" (Phase 1, cleared in 1988.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), map 「城寨分三期清拆的分區略圖」 | INFERRED: On the map the line between Phase 1 and Phase 2 is labelled Tai Chang Street (大井街), and the line between Phase 2 and Phase 3 is labelled Lo Yan Street (老人街). The labels are small; I read them from the scan. | "城寨分三期清拆的分區略圖" (Sketch map of the City's three clearance phases.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), map 「城寨分三期清拆的分區略圖」 | INFERRED: The map names the roads around the City: Tung Tau Tsuen Road (東頭邨道) to the north (with Mei Tung Estate (美東邨) beyond it), 西城道 to the west, Tung Ching Road (東正道) to the east and Lung Chun Road (龍津道) to the south. | "城寨分三期清拆的分區略圖" (Sketch map of the City's three clearance phases.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), article 「城寨福利會陳協平稱賠償方案可以接受」 | Chan Hip Ping (陳協平), vice-chairman of the 九龍城寨街坊福利會, said on 10 December 1987 that the plan basically matched the wishes of most residents and was acceptable. | "基本上符合城寨大多數居民的意願，是可以接受的" (Basically matches the wishes of most City residents, and is acceptable.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), article 「城寨福利會陳協平稱賠償方案可以接受」 | The association held an enlarged meeting of its executive committee and a press conference in the afternoon. | "舉行了街坊福利會執行委員會的擴大會議" (Held an enlarged meeting of the kaifong association's executive committee.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), article 「城寨福利會陳協平稱賠償方案可以接受」 | Chan Hip Ping (陳協平) said some matters still needed talks: care for the old and those without support, the future of industry and commerce, compensation for well-placed shops, and the future practice of the City's doctors and dentists. | "對城寨醫生、牙科的日後執業出路等問題" (On problems such as the future practice of City doctors and dentists.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), article 「五千個公屋單位為城寨居民準備」 | 呂孝端 said the City then had about 10,000 households and about 30,000 residents. | "城寨現時約有一萬戶約三萬名居民" (The City now has about 10,000 households and about 30,000 residents.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), article 「五千個公屋單位為城寨居民準備」 | The Housing Authority had kept more than 5,000 urban public housing units and more than 1,000 near-urban units over the next five years for City residents. | "預留五千多個市區公屋單位及一千多個接近市區的公屋單位" (Reserved 5,000-odd urban public housing units and 1,000-odd near-urban public housing units.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), article 「五千個公屋單位為城寨居民準備」 | 呂孝端 said cash at the value of an HOS flat was the most costly option of the plan. | "以相當於居屋市值的現金補償方法最為昂貴" (Cash compensation equal to the HOS market value is the most expensive.) |
| NPTKP19871211, leaf n4 (港聞 page), photo caption, right | The photos show Hu Fa-kuang (胡法光), 呂孝端 and 浦炳榮 announcing the plan, and the chairman of the 九龍城寨街坊福利會, 李偉羣, with vice-chairman Chan Hip Ping (陳協平) at their press conference. | "九龍城寨街坊福利會會長李偉羣（右三）及副會長陳協平（右二）" (Kowloon Walled City Kaifong Welfare Association chairman 李偉羣 (third from right) and vice-chairman 陳協平 (second from right).) |

- **Leads:**
  - Compensation and rehousing criteria of the Housing Authority special committee for the City, announced 10 Dec 1987 (cited by Ta Kung Pao (大公報) 11 Dec 1987, leaf n4).
  - Legislative Council Finance Committee approval of HK$2,760 million for the City, December 1987 (cited by Ta Kung Pao (大公報) 11 Dec 1987, leaf n4).
### zh-press-10: 清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償 批出補償總額二十七億餘元

- **Status:** Partly read on 2026-11-17; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-19. I read the page scans as images (archive.org BookReader image service, full resolution, cropped and enlarged in the Playwright browser; no file saved to the repo). On leaf n4 I read the lead article, the kaifong statement, the article on the appeal panels and the tape, and most of the short item on small owners (its end runs into a column that I did not read). Leaf n6 holds the full text of the special committee's statement and booklet (補償及安置資格, 上訴途徑, 工商業用途樓宇, 住戶及營商人士的補償, 可計算樓面面積的定義 and more), in three tiers across the page. I read only its heading and three short blocks; the rest is not read. Correction to the finder's note: the item '居民與警對峙 清拆行動暫緩' (leaf n5) is about the clearance of 竹園聯合村, not the City. The taped explanation was played at the clearance office in Block 17, Lok Fu Estate, not in the City.
- **Citation:** 華僑日報 (Wah Kiu Yat Po), 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償 / 批出補償總額二十七億餘元 發通知信首批居民明年遷出」 and related items, 11 December 1987, 第二張第一頁 (本港新聞), archive.org item NPWK19871211, leaf n4; with the statement 「清拆事宜特別委會公布 九龍城寨居民補償事宜」 on leaf n6. https://archive.org/details/NPWK19871211
- **Class:** primary (News reports of the press conference of 10 December 1987 by the chairman of the special committee, with the kaifong association's statement and the official compensation text, made at the time (1987). Primary tree is correct.)
- **Date of the record:** 1987-12-11
- **Files:**
  - `zh-press-10-NPWK19871211_jp2_NPWK19871211_0004.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Full-resolution page image of leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), the page with the lead article and the related City items, as archive.org serves it in JPEG from the JP2 scan.; https://archive.org/download/NPWK19871211/NPWK19871211_jp2.zip/NPWK19871211_jp2%2FNPWK19871211_0004.jp2&ext=jpg; 2198224 bytes; SHA-256 `8f96134511d771904d899f02464ef5a26702744145d621b1ba2c41e0215641bc`; licence: No rights statement on the archive.org item (its metadata fields rights and licenseurl are empty; it is in the collection china-mail, from a private uploader, with no source named). The newspaper is copyright of its publisher, 華僑日報 (Wah Kiu Yat Po). (https://archive.org/details/NPWK19871211)
  - `zh-press-10-NPWK19871211_jp2_NPWK19871211_0006.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Full-resolution page image of leaf n6 (第二張第三頁), the full text of the special committee's statement on compensation (only partly read), as archive.org serves it in JPEG from the JP2 scan.; https://archive.org/download/NPWK19871211/NPWK19871211_jp2.zip/NPWK19871211_jp2%2FNPWK19871211_0006.jp2&ext=jpg; 2536919 bytes; SHA-256 `3c00c56c348886167b6f90ebf5fbe9be4b96d113db4273f39d90c7854fe37528`; licence: No rights statement on the archive.org item (its metadata fields rights and licenseurl are empty; it is in the collection china-mail, from a private uploader, with no source named). The newspaper is copyright of its publisher, 華僑日報 (Wah Kiu Yat Po). (https://archive.org/details/NPWK19871211)

| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, first tier, right | 胡法光, chairman of the special committee on the City clearance, said that the LegCo Finance Committee had approved over HK$2.76 billion for the clearance, in three years. | "立法局財務委員會已就九龍城寨清拆事宜批出逾廿七億六千萬元，分三年清拆" (The LegCo Finance Committee has approved more than 2.76 billion dollars for the Kowloon Walled City clearance, to be cleared over three years.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, first tier, right | INFERRED: He spoke at a press conference on 10 December 1987. The first notice letters were to go out at the end of December 1987. The date comes from 'yesterday'. | "胡法光昨日在記者招待會上指出" (胡法光 said at a press conference yesterday.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, first tier, right | The first batch of residents was to move out from early to late 1988, the second from late 1988 to 1989, and the third from early to late 1990. | "九龍城寨第一批居民在八八年初至八八年底搬出，第二批在八八年底至八九年，第三批在九〇年初至九〇年底" (The first batch of City residents moves out from early to end 1988, the second from end 1988 to 1989, the third from early to end 1990.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, first tier, right | More than 3,000 letters on compensation and rehousing were to go to the first-phase residents in batches, at the end of the month and over the Lunar New Year. | "因為發出的專函超過三千封，所以必須分批處理及發出" (Because more than 3,000 letters are sent, they must be handled and sent in batches.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, first tier | Public rental flats for City owners and tenants were offered in Kowloon, Hong Kong Island, Sha Tin, Tseung Kwan O, Tsuen Wan and Tsing Yi. | "租住公屋地點包括九龍、港島、沙田、將軍澳、荃灣及青衣" (Public rental housing locations include Kowloon, Hong Kong Island, Sha Tin, Tseung Kwan O, Tsuen Wan and Tsing Yi.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, first tier | Home Ownership and private-sector scheme flats: in 1988-89 in Kowloon, Sha Tin and Ma On Shan, Tseung Kwan O, Tsuen Wan, Tuen Mun and Tai Po; later years added Siu Sai Wan, Chai Wan, Tsing Yi and Fanling. | "八八至八九年選擇地點包括九龍、沙田及馬鞍山、將軍澳、荃灣、屯門、大埔" (Locations to choose from in 1988-89 include Kowloon, Sha Tin and Ma On Shan, Tseung Kwan O, Tsuen Wan, Tuen Mun and Tai Po.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, top left, '一、自住業主方面' | The committee decided against flat-for-flat compensation for owner-occupiers: City property prices were low, and the committee thought flat-for-flat too generous. Compensation was based on the price of Home Ownership flats. | "鑑於城寨物業價格偏低，假如要資助業主購買私人樓宇，此舉會被認為過份慷慨" (Since City property prices are low, subsidising owners to buy private flats would be seen as too generous.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, top left, second column block | The rate per square metre of countable floor area: HK$9,670 up to 23.44 m², HK$6,930 from 23.45 to 60 m², and HK$5,000 above 60 m². | "樓宇認可面積在廿三點四四平方米以下，每平方米為九千六百七十元" (For a flat with a recognised area below 23.44 square metres, it is 9,670 dollars per square metre.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, top left, second column block | Thus compensation ran from HK$89,836 for the smallest unit, 100 sq ft, to HK$923,243 for the largest, 1,600 sq ft. Most units in the City were about 23.44 m², and that size was the basis of the scale. (The print has '一百平方呎米', a misprint for 一百平方呎.) | "由最小面積單位一百平方呎米，獲得八萬九千八百卅六元，至最大之單位面積一千六百平方呎，獲得九十二萬三千二百四十三元不等" (From 89,836 dollars for the smallest unit of 100 sq ft, to 923,243 dollars for the largest unit of 1,600 sq ft.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, top left, '二、業權方面' | Because owners lacked title deeds, the Registrar General set special rules, approved by the committee, to decide who held title. Owner-occupiers and non-resident owners got the same cash compensation. | "鑑於缺乏契約文件，在確定及核證城寨樓宇業權資格遇上困難" (Because contract documents are lacking, there are difficulties in confirming and verifying the title of City buildings.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, top left, '三、安置資格方面' | All residents who really lived in the City on and after 14 January 1987 could get permanent rehousing, if they had lived in Hong Kong for seven years in a row by the end of March 1990. | "所有在一九八七年一月十四日當日及其後真正居住在九龍城寨的居民均可獲永久安置資格" (All residents really living in Kowloon Walled City on and after 14 January 1987 can get permanent rehousing.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, top left, '三、安置資格方面' | Eligible tenants could choose a public rental flat, priority to buy a Home Ownership flat, or an interest-free loan under a future home purchase loan scheme. | "符合資格的租客，可選擇租住公屋單位，或自資優先購置居屋" (Eligible tenants can choose to rent a public housing unit, or buy an HOS flat with priority using their own money.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, top left, '四、營商人士方面' | The committee drew a line between trades: lawful trades such as food factories could get compensation, but illegal ones such as vice establishments and gambling stalls could not. | "例如食品工廠這些應可獲得補償資格的行業，便應有別於其他非法行業，如色情場所及賭檔" (Trades that qualify for compensation, such as food factories, must be kept apart from other illegal trades such as vice establishments and gambling stalls.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, top left, '四、營商人士方面' | No compensation for people who moved in after 14 January 1987, or who after that date split units, built new floors, enlarged buildings or started a business. Owners who bought after that date got only fair market value. | "或將原有樓宇面積分間，或興建新樓，或擴建原有樓宇面積，或新設營商業務，皆不會獲得任何補償" (Or who subdivided existing floor area, built new storeys, enlarged a building, or started a new business, will get no compensation at all.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, second tier, '五、志願團體方面' | Five voluntary bodies worked in the City. The Social Welfare Department found new premises elsewhere for those it funded; those without government funding got no new premises but other help. | "城寨內共有五個志願團體" (There are five voluntary bodies in the City in all.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, second tier, '六、商業性水供水商' | The City's water suppliers (城寨供水商) were to keep supplying water until all residents had moved out. | "城寨供水商會將繼續供水服務，直至所有居民遷出城寨為止" (The City's water suppliers will keep supplying water until all residents have moved out of the City.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, second tier, '七、社會個案' | Staff of the City and New Territories Administration and the Social Welfare Department had visited old people and others in the City who needed help. | "政務總署及社會福利署職員已探訪城寨老人家及其他需要幫助的人士" (Staff of the City and New Territories Administration and the Social Welfare Department have visited old people and others in the City who need help.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, second tier, right | A working group under the committee was studying the future of the City's unregistered doctors and dentists; the matter was complex and needed time. Committee member 浦炳榮 chaired it. | "特委會屬下工作小組現正就城寨註冊醫生及牙醫的日後安排，加緊研究可行辦法" (A working group under the committee is stepping up study of workable arrangements for the future of the City's (non-)registered doctors and dentists.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, second tier, right | 胡法光 said that dentists and doctors who owned their shop units would get compensation, with business loss counted. | "經營牙科診所或醫療的人士，若擁有該座舖位，則可獲得賠償與及按照商業損失計算在內" (People who run dental clinics or medical practices, if they own the shop unit, can get compensation, with business loss counted in.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4 (第二張第一頁, 本港新聞), lead article 「清拆九龍城寨補償細則公布 三年分批清拆 業主均獲補償」, second tier | 錢茂林, liaison officer of the medical group of the kaifong association, asked for the arrangements to be published two months before each phase. He said that the doctors' working group was formed too late. | "希望在首期遷拆之前兩個月公佈安排細節" (Hopes the details of the arrangements are published two months before the first phase of clearance.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4, article 「城寨坊會發表聲明 補償方案符合意願可以接受」, first part | The kaifong association held an executive meeting on the morning of 10 December 1987. Its spokesman 陳協平 said that the plan met the wishes of most City residents and was acceptable. | "更認為此方案基本上符合城寨大多數居民意願，及可以接受" (It further considers that the plan basically meets the wishes of most City residents and is acceptable.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4, article 「城寨坊會發表聲明 補償方案符合意願可以接受」, second part | 陳協平 said that the association and other residents had gone among the residents to coordinate and collect their wishes, and that committee members and officials had visited the City many times and held forums. | "亦多次到城寨探訪居民，了解實情，並舉行座談" (They also came to the City many times to visit residents, learn the facts and hold discussions.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4, article 「設立兩個小組接受申訴 錄音帶講賠償方案不斷播放協助了解」, right part | The special clearance office in Block 17, Lok Fu Estate began to answer questions on the plan on 10 December 1987. Many residents came at noon, and staff gave out booklets. | "位於樂富邨十七座的專責辦事處，昨日中午已有大批城寨居民前往該處諮詢" (At the special office in Block 17 Lok Fu Estate, many City residents went to ask questions yesterday at noon.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4, article 「設立兩個小組接受申訴 錄音帶講賠償方案不斷播放協助了解」, middle | A tape that explained the plan in Cantonese and Chiu Chow (潮語) was played in office hours. | "一套以廣東話及潮語講解賠償方案的錄影帶，在辦公時間播放" (A tape explaining the compensation plan in Cantonese and Chiu Chow is played in office hours.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4, article 「設立兩個小組接受申訴 錄音帶講賠償方案不斷播放協助了解」, left part | Two appeal panels: an appeal tribunal chaired by committee member 梁煒彤 for all clearance complaints (not valuations or complaints against civil servants), and a valuation review panel chaired by 簡福貽. | "由特委會委員梁煒彤担任主席的上訴審裁小組" (The appeal tribunal panel chaired by committee member 梁煒彤.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4, article 「設立兩個小組接受申訴 錄音帶講賠償方案不斷播放協助了解」, left end | Countable floor area meant the floor area of a unit that the owner could use. It did not count iron cages, cocklofts, stairs, passages, lift shafts, pigeon lofts, dog houses, chicken coops, water tanks, pump rooms, transformer rooms, meter rooms, garages, open roofs, balconies, gardens or platforms. | "但不包括附屬的鐵籠、閣仔及公用地方如樓梯、通道、升降機槽、門廊，亦不包括鴿籠、狗屋、雞棚、水箱、泵房" (But not including attached iron cages, cocklofts and common areas such as stairs, passages, lift shafts and porches, nor pigeon lofts, dog houses, chicken coops, water tanks or pump rooms.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n4, short item 「城寨小業主多滿意 歡迎賠償安置方法」, lower right, right part | Most owners and shop operators welcomed the terms. Some small owners were satisfied but would not buy Home Ownership flats in the New Territories, because they were used to living in Kowloon. | "但他們將不會接受安排購買新界的居屋，因為住慣了九龍" (But they will not accept buying HOS flats in the New Territories, because they are used to living in Kowloon.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n6 (第二張第三頁), notice text 「清拆事宜特別委會公布 九龍城寨居民補償事宜」, first block 「補償及安置資格」 | To qualify for rehousing, a person had to be a lawful Hong Kong resident, registered in the pre-clearance survey of 14 January 1987 and really living in the City, and have lived in Hong Kong seven years by 31 March 1990. | "你必須於一九九〇年三月三十一日止在香港居住滿七年" (You must have lived in Hong Kong for seven years by 31 March 1990.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n6 (第二張第三頁), notice text 「清拆事宜特別委會公布 九龍城寨居民補償事宜」, block 「住戶及營商人士的補償」 | Movers who paid surveyors or valuers could get their reasonable fees back. Eligible households and businesses could also claim reasonable removal costs, such as moving furniture, plant and machinery, and legal and stamp fees for a new flat. | "例如搬遷家具、廠房及機器的費用、購置新樓宇所需的律師費及印花稅等" (For example the cost of moving furniture, plant and machinery, and the legal fees and stamp duty to buy a new flat.) |
| NPWK19871211, leaf n6 (第二張第三頁), notice text 「清拆事宜特別委會公布 九龍城寨居民補償事宜」, block 「工商業用途樓宇」 | The compensation rule for industrial and commercial buildings was the same as for residential ones, but their owners or tenants usually could not get rehousing. | "惟一分別是工商業樓宇擁有人或租用者一般皆不能獲得安置" (The only difference is that owners or tenants of industrial and commercial buildings generally cannot be rehoused.) |

- **Leads:**
  - The rest of the special committee's compensation statement on leaf n6 of this same issue (補償及安置資格, 上訴途徑, 住戶及營商人士的補償 and more): printed in full, but I read only its heading and three blocks.
  - The compensation and rehousing booklet and the notice letters of December 1987 to the first-phase residents (not held).
  - The LegCo Finance Committee approval of over HK$2.76 billion for the City clearance, 1987 (not held).
  - The taped explanation of the plan in Cantonese and Chiu Chow (潮語) played at the clearance office in Block 17, Lok Fu Estate, December 1987 (not held).
### gap-clearance-from-below-11: 大公報 1988-02-13: 對補償及安置問題覆函顯示城寨業主多選擇居屋住客則最多選擇公屋三戶人家昨成為城寨首批遷出居民

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-17; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-19.
- **Citation:** Ta Kung Pao (大公報), 「對補償及安置問題覆函顯示 城寨業主多選擇居屋 住客則最多選擇公屋」 with 「三戶人家昨成為城寨首批遷出居民」, 13 February 1988, local news page, archive.org item NPTKP19880213, leaf n5, https://archive.org/details/NPTKP19880213/page/n5
- **Class:** primary (A newspaper report of the next day on statements of the official in charge of the clearance (1988). It belongs in primary-sources/.)
- **Date of the record:** 1988-02-13
- **Files:**
  - `gap-clearance-from-below-11-BookReaderImages.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Full-resolution JPEG of leaf n5 of the archive.org scan of 大公報 13 Feb 1988 (the page with the article), about 4-6 MB. Item page: https://archive.org/details/NPTKP19880213/page/n5; https://ia600502.us.archive.org/BookReader/BookReaderImages.php?zip=/21/items/NPTKP19880213/NPTKP19880213_jp2.zip&file=NPTKP19880213_jp2/NPTKP19880213_0005.jp2&id=NPTKP19880213&scale=1&rotate=0; 4724767 bytes; SHA-256 `352c90e1eb82443ca4480d041d0f582711c647f90795c24e94006bf1901b5f14`; licence: No rights statement: the archive.org item has empty 'rights' and 'licenseurl' fields (uploaded by a private person, collections 'china-mail' and 'newspapers'). Copyright of the publisher. (https://archive.org/details/NPTKP19880213)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| NPTKP19880213, leaf n5 (港聞 page), centre | INFERRED: 呂孝端, Deputy Secretary (副政務司), said on 12 February 1988 that the government had sent 2,500 letters to residents of the City with the details of compensation and rehousing. | "當局至今已向九龍城寨居民發出二千五百封函件" (The authorities have so far sent 2,500 letters to Kowloon Walled City residents.) |
| NPTKP19880213, leaf n5 (港聞 page), centre | Among the residents who replied, owner-occupiers mostly chose Home Ownership Scheme (居屋) flats, and tenants mostly chose public rental housing. | "自住業主較多選擇居屋，而住客則最多選擇入住公屋" (Owner-occupiers more often chose HOS flats, while tenants most often chose to live in public housing.) |
| NPTKP19880213, leaf n5 (港聞 page), centre | On 12 February 1988 three households of the City moved to public housing, with the help of several government departments. They were the first residents to move out since the clearance was announced. | "九龍城寨三戶居民昨日在港府多個部門協助下，搬往公屋居住，成為自宣佈清拆城寨以來首批遷出的居民" (Three households of Kowloon Walled City yesterday moved to public housing with the help of several government departments, the first to move out since the clearance was announced.) |
| NPTKP19880213, leaf n5 (港聞 page), centre | The letters were to reach 3,000 before the Lunar New Year, and the last 1,000 were to go out by the end of February 1988. | "在農曆新年前發出有關函件將達三千封，至二月底將發出最後的一千封" (Before the Lunar New Year the letters sent will reach 3,000; by the end of February the last 1,000 will go out.) |
| NPTKP19880213, leaf n5 (港聞 page), centre | Of 1,500 replies received, owner-occupiers chose, in this order: an HOS flat, a public rental flat, a cheque at fair market value, and a cheque with a home-purchase loan. | "最多是選擇居屋單位，其次是公屋，再次是以公平市值取支票及取支票加申請購屋貸款" (Most chose an HOS flat, next public housing, then a cheque at fair market value, and a cheque plus a home-purchase loan.) |
| NPTKP19880213, leaf n5 (港聞 page), centre | Tenants chose, in this order: a public rental flat, an HOS flat bought with their own money, a cheque, and a cheque with a home-purchase loan. | "住客的選擇則最多為申請租住公屋，其次是自資買居屋" (Tenants mostly chose to apply to rent public housing, next to buy an HOS flat with their own money.) |
| NPTKP19880213, leaf n5 (港聞 page), centre | The special-duties office (專責事務處) had 283 cheques ready, for a total of more than HK$52.7 million. That is about HK$186,000 a cheque on average (my calculation). | "專責事務處已準備好二百八十三張支票，款項達五千二百七十多萬元" (The special duties office has prepared 283 cheques, for over 52.7 million dollars.) |
| NPTKP19880213, leaf n5 (港聞 page), centre | The first moving period was 1988 to mid-1989, the second 1989, and the third 1990. | "第一個搬遷期為八八至八九年中，第二期為八九年，第三期為九○年" (The first moving period is 1988 to mid-1989, the second 1989, the third 1990.) |
| NPTKP19880213, leaf n5 (港聞 page), centre | 浦炳榮, a member of the special committee on the clearance, said that the study of the removal and compensation of the City's dentists and doctors was going well. Its first proposals were due in March 1988. | "有關研究城寨牙醫及醫生的搬遷及補償問題，進展非常順利，初步建議將在三月完成" (The study of the removal and compensation of the City's dentists and doctors is going very smoothly; preliminary proposals will be finished in March.) |

- **Leads:**
  - The compensation and rehousing letters of the clearance office to City residents (2,500 by 12 February 1988) and the residents' reply forms (not held).
  - The proposals of 浦炳榮's study on the City's dentists and doctors, due March 1988 (the repo holds the clinic compensation report of 4 September 1988, in NPWK19880904; the March 1988 proposals are not held).
### zh-press-11: 九龍城寨清拆賠償首名商戶交出舖位空置單位將閂為護衛員休息室清拆計劃公佈後城寨竟成外來客旅遊重點

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-20; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-20.
- **Citation:** 華僑日報 (Wah Kiu Yat Po), 「九龍城寨清拆賠償 首名商戶交出舖位 空置單位將闢為護衛員休息室 清拆計劃公佈後城寨竟成外來客旅遊重點」, 15 March 1988, local news page (本港新聞), archive.org item NPWK19880315, leaf n5, https://archive.org/details/NPWK19880315/page/n5
- **Class:** primary (A news report of an interview with the official in charge, made the day after (1988). It belongs in primary-sources/.)
- **Date of the record:** 1988-03-15
- **Files:**
  - `zh-press-11-NPWK19880315_jp2_NPWK19880315_0005.jp2_ext_jpg-65cabbaa.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Full-resolution page image of leaf n5 (本港新聞), the page with the article, as archive.org serves it in JPEG from the JP2 scan; https://archive.org/download/NPWK19880315/NPWK19880315_jp2.zip/NPWK19880315_jp2%2FNPWK19880315_0005.jp2&ext=jpg; 2129618 bytes; SHA-256 `f04bcee0e6a09c3a46f28da5d89305c67e1442196acee557bed82764517b5e9f`; licence: No rights statement on the archive.org item (its metadata has no rights or licenseurl field; it is in the collection china-mail, from a private uploader). The newspaper is copyright of its publisher, 華僑日報 (Wah Kiu Yat Po). (https://archive.org/details/NPWK19880315)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | 呂孝端, 副政務司（專責事務）, told the paper that the first shop owner in the City had accepted the compensation sum of the special committee. | "九龍城寨第一個商戶已經接納特別委員會訂出的賠償金額" (The first shop owner in Kowloon Walled City has accepted the compensation sum set by the special committee.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | INFERRED: The owner took the cash on 14 March 1988 and at once gave the shop back to the Buildings and Lands Department (屋宇地政署). The date comes from "yesterday" in the paper of 15 March. | "業主昨日領取現金後立即將舖位交回屋宇地政署" (Yesterday the owner took the cash and at once handed the shop back to the Buildings and Lands Department.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | The shop was at 42 Sai Shing Road inside the City; the paper prints the street as 西城道, not 西城路. The owner had planned to run a restaurant (飯店) in it. | "這個店舖是位於城寨內街西城道四十二號，業主原本是將此舖位經營飯店" (This shop is at 42 Sai Shing Road, an inner street of the City; the owner had planned to run a restaurant there.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | The fitting-out of the shop was still in progress when the clearance was announced (January 1987). Thus the restaurant never opened. | "但因裝修期間遇上公佈城寨清拆，故一直未曾營業過" (But the clearance was announced during the fitting-out, so it never did business.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | The shop floor was about 172.7 square metres. Its compensation was HK$1.04 million. | "此地面積約有一百七十二點七平方米，可獲的賠償額為一百零四萬元" (The floor area is about 172.7 square metres; the compensation is 1.04 million dollars.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | The special committee studied the shops that did no business first. Government property surveyors were then computing the sums for several similar shops. | "特別委員會會先研究一些無營業的商戶，現時正由政府產業測計師算數間類似的商戶店舖" (The special committee will first study some shops that did no business; government property surveyors are now computing several similar shops.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | From April 1988 the empty ground-floor unit at 42 Sai Shing Road was to be a rest place and halfway station for the guards (護衛員). Later it was to be a work room for the staff who seal the vacated units. | "會在下月起開為「護衛員」休息地點及中途站，稍後更將其用作工作間" (From next month it will be opened as a rest place and halfway station for the "guards", and later used as a work room.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | By 14 March 1988 the government had sent 3,413 compensation and rehousing letters for phase 1 of the clearance, and 2,970 had come back. | "城寨第一期的清拆補償安置信共發出三千四百一十三封，現已收回二千九百七十封" (For phase 1 of the clearance, 3,413 compensation and rehousing letters were sent; 2,970 have come back.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | The paper says that 99 per cent of the residents accepted the compensation proposals of the special committee. The base of the percentage (all letters or the replies) is not clear. | "達百分之九十九居民願意接納特別委員會的補償建議" (Up to 99 per cent of residents are willing to accept the compensation proposals of the special committee.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | Letters went to 60 shops of the City. More than 50 of these shops accepted the move and the compensation allowance. | "在發給六十間城寨商戶的信件中，有五十多間商戶接納搬遷及補償津貼" (Of the letters sent to 60 City shops, more than 50 shops accepted removal and the compensation allowance.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | About 15 per cent of the households had filled in forms to buy Home Ownership Scheme (居屋) flats. | "目前有一成半住戶填表申請購買居屋" (At present 15 per cent of households have filled in forms to apply to buy HOS flats.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | The Housing Department planned to offer Home Ownership Scheme flats in Tseung Kwan O (將軍澳) in early April 1988. The City office, the Housing Department and the kaifong association 城寨街坊會 arranged free coaches to take residents to see them. | "免費安排旅遊車接載城寨居民前往參觀" (Free coaches are arranged to carry City residents there to visit.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | Home Ownership Scheme flats in the urban area were also to be offered before the end of 1988. City residents could choose them too. | "市區內的居屋也在本年底前推出，城寨居民亦可考慮" (HOS flats in the urban area will also be offered before the end of this year; City residents can also consider them.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | 呂孝端 said again that the site would become a park after the clearance. The first draft of the park design was almost complete. | "此地一定會開闢為公園，有關公園的設計發展藍圖初稿亦即將完成" (This site will certainly become a park; the first draft of the park design is about to be finished.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | After the clearance was announced, the City became a main sight for visitors from outside. Almost each day many visitors came to see it inside and outside. | "竟成為外地旅客的遊覽重點，幾乎每日都吸引很多遊客到城寨內外參觀" (It has become a main sight for visitors from elsewhere; almost every day it draws many visitors to see the inside and outside of the City.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | INFERRED: On 6 February 1988, Dr 許舒 led more than 100 members of the 「香港皇家亞洲學院」 (as the paper names it) to the City to collect material. The group included the financial secretary 林定國. The date comes from "the 6th of last month". | "上月六日由許舒博士率領的百多位「香港皇家亞洲學院」的成員也會到此找資料" (On the 6th of last month, more than 100 members of the 「香港皇家亞洲學院」 led by Dr 許舒 also came here to collect material.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | No method of demolition was fixed yet. Five international companies had sent demolition plans to the Buildings and Lands Department, for example demolition by hand and inward central implosion. | "已有五間國際性的公司向屋宇地政署提供清拆計劃書" (Five international companies have already sent demolition proposals to the Buildings and Lands Department.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | By 14 March 1988 the government had recovered 24 domestic units. From April 1988 these and the other vacated units were to be sealed with bricks for safety. | "至今已收回二十四個住宅單位，下月開始會連同一併遷出的單位用磚塊加封" (So far 24 domestic units are recovered; from next month they and the other vacated units will be sealed with bricks.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | INFERRED: At the special committee meeting of Thursday 17 March 1988, the working group of 浦炳榮 was to report orally on the compensation of the unlicensed dentists and doctors. The full report was due at the end of April 1988. The scan reads 中醫 (Chinese-medicine doctors); other reports name the group for the unregistered western doctors and dentists, thus this reading is not certain. | "報告即將完成的城寨無牌牙醫及中醫的補償進度" (It will report the progress of the compensation of the unlicensed dentists and doctors of the City, whose report is nearly complete.) |
| NPWK19880315, leaf n5 (本港新聞), right half, middle tier, box 「人物專訪」 and the article under the ferry-collision story | Many households were to move out in April and May 1988, and the car spaces on Tung Tau Tsuen Road (東頭村道) and Tung Tsing Road (東正道) were too few. The Urban Council (市政局) agreed to make four basketball courts on Carpenter Road (賈炳達道) a temporary car park for the removal vehicles. | "市政局已同意將位於賈炳達道的四個籃球場，開闢為臨時停車場" (The Urban Council has agreed to open the four basketball courts on Carpenter Road as a temporary car park.) |

- **Leads:**
  - The visit of the 「香港皇家亞洲學院」 led by Dr 許舒 to the City on 6 February 1988 (WKYP 15 March 1988, leaf n5). Compare the James Hayes papers (HKMS169-2-1) in the repo, which name a later RAS visit of 6 May 1989.
  - The demolition proposals of five international companies, sent to the Buildings and Lands Department before March 1988 (not held).
  - The oral report of the working group of 浦炳榮 on the unlicensed doctors and dentists to the special committee, 17 March 1988, and its full report of April 1988 (not held).
  - The first draft of the park design for the City site, early 1988 (not held).
### top-zh-press-15: 環境稠密通道狹窄灌救困難城寨小火列為四級消防員救出逾百人燒臘店嚴重焚毀

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-17; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-19.
- **Citation:** 大公報 (Ta Kung Pao), 「環境稠密通道狹窄灌救困難 城寨小火列為四級 消防員救出逾百人 燒臘店嚴重焚毀」, 20 April 1988, 港聞, 第七版, archive.org item NPTKP19880420, leaf n6, https://archive.org/details/NPTKP19880420/page/n6
- **Class:** primary (A news report and a press photo of a fire of the day before (1988). Primary tree.)
- **Date of the record:** 1988-04-20 (the fire was on the morning of 19 April 1988)
- **Files:**
  - `top-zh-press-15-BookReaderImages.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Full-resolution page image of leaf n6 (港聞 第七版), with the article and the photo of the fire, JPEG from the archive.org BookReader image service at scale=1 (about 3500 x 4800 px). If the server name in the URL changes, the same leaf at half resolution is at https://archive.org/download/NPTKP19880420/NPTKP19880420_jp2.zip/NPTKP19880420_jp2%2FNPTKP19880420_0006.jp2&ext=jpg .; https://ia800709.us.archive.org/BookReader/BookReaderImages.php?zip=/30/items/NPTKP19880420/NPTKP19880420_jp2.zip&file=NPTKP19880420_jp2/NPTKP19880420_0006.jp2&id=NPTKP19880420&scale=1&rotate=0; 4129580 bytes; SHA-256 `48d00be572b32f3bc5f2d8e267d4e90905ed38f2851e68fc3b24871419d69fef`; licence: No rights statement on the archive.org item (its metadata fields rights and licenseurl are empty). The newspaper is copyright of its publisher, 大公報 (Ta Kung Pao). (https://archive.org/details/NPTKP19880420)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| NPTKP19880420, leaf n6 (港聞, 第七版), centre | The fire was in a roast-meat shop (燒臘店) on the ground floor of 10A Lung Chun Back Road (龍津後街), in the City. | "火警現場為城寨龍津後街十號A地下一間燒臘店" (The scene of the fire was a roast-meat shop on the ground floor of 10A Lung Chun Back Road in the City.) |
| NPTKP19880420, leaf n6 (港聞, 第七版), centre | The shop roasted its meat with liquefied petroleum gas (石油氣), and it usually kept many gas cylinders. | "由於使用石油氣燒烤，平日置有不少的罐裝氣體" (Because it roasted with LPG, it usually kept many canisters of gas.) |
| NPTKP19880420, leaf n6 (港聞, 第七版), centre | The fire started at 7:31 am on 19 April 1988 inside the shop. Two workers tried to put it out, but failed, and ran out to report it because of the thick smoke. | "昨晨七時三十一分，燒臘店內突然起火，兩名工人曾試圖撲救" (At 7:31 yesterday morning, a fire suddenly broke out in the roast-meat shop; two workers tried to put it out.) |
| NPTKP19880420, leaf n6 (港聞, 第七版), centre | The fire itself was not large. But the City was dense and its paths winding, thus the Fire Services raised it to a No. 4 alarm to get more men. | "火勢雖然不大，但由於城內環境稠密，路徑曲折" (Although the fire was not large, because the City was dense and its paths winding,) |
| NPTKP19880420, leaf n6 (港聞, 第七版), centre | Residents nearby ran up to the roofs (天台), and there was confusion. | "附近居民紛紛逃上天台，情況混亂" (Residents nearby fled one after another up to the roof; the situation was chaotic.) |
| NPTKP19880420, leaf n6 (港聞, 第七版), centre | Firemen raised the fire to a No. 3 alarm ten minutes after arrival, and to a No. 4 alarm five minutes after that. | "在十分鐘後便將火升為三級，再於五分鐘後升為四級" (After ten minutes raised the fire to No. 3, and after five more minutes to No. 4.) |
| NPTKP19880420, leaf n6 (港聞, 第七版), centre | Firemen led more than 100 trapped residents out. Nobody was hurt. The fire was out at 8:40 am. | "超過一百名居民要由消防員救出" (More than one hundred residents had to be rescued by firemen.) |
| NPTKP19880420, leaf n6 (港聞, 第七版), centre | The shop was badly burnt. Several flats above it and next to it were also damaged. | "起火燒臘店嚴重焚毀，樓上及鄰近多個單位亦受到波及" (The roast-meat shop was badly burnt; several units above and nearby were also affected.) |
| NPTKP19880420, leaf n6 (港聞, 第七版), centre | 侯雄志, 九龍中區高級消防區長 (a senior fire officer of Kowloon Central), gave the reasons for the No. 4 alarm: the special living conditions, the winding passages, and residents who waited for rescue on the roofs. | "通道迂迴曲折，加上有居民在天台等候救援，故升為四級" (The passages were winding, and residents were waiting on the roof for rescue, thus it was raised to No. 4.) |
| NPTKP19880420, leaf n6 (港聞, 第七版), centre, photo below the article | A press photo shows firemen in a narrow passage, with hoses on the floor and cables and pipes overhead. The caption says that firemen had to fight the fire in such a narrow space. The description of the photo is mine. | "城寨四級火警，消防員要在這樣狹窄的環境下灌救" (No. 4 alarm fire in the Walled City: firemen had to fight the fire in such narrow surroundings.) |

- **Leads:**
  - 華僑日報 (Wah Kiu Yat Po), 20 April 1988, archive.org item NPWK19880420: can hold a report of the same fire (not read).
  - The Fire Services Department incident record of the No. 4 alarm fire at 10A Lung Chun Back Road (龍津後街), 19 April 1988 (not held).
### top-zh-press-16: 男童十二樓墮下五樓花架救一命城寨老人街昨日出現驚險鏡頭

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-17; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-19.
- **Citation:** 大公報 (Ta Kung Pao), 「男童十二樓墮下五樓花架救一命 城寨老人街昨日出現驚險鏡頭」, 1 July 1988, 港聞, 第五版, archive.org item NPTKP19880701, leaf n4, https://archive.org/details/NPTKP19880701/page/n4
- **Class:** primary (A news report of an accident of the day before (1988). Primary tree.)
- **Date of the record:** 1988-07-01 (the accident was at 3:30 pm on 30 June 1988)
- **Files:**
  - `top-zh-press-16-BookReaderImages.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Full-resolution page image of leaf n4 (港聞 第五版), the page with the article, JPEG from the archive.org BookReader image service at scale=1 (about 3500 x 4800 px).
